### Müşteri listesi fiyat sütunları (opsiyonel, aç/kapa)
`Customers.jsx`'in **Müşteriler** tablosuna (yalnız `isCustomer`, Bayilerde değil) dört opsiyonel fiyat sütunu eklenebilir: **Fatura Bedeli** (`faturaBedeliOf(c)`), **Fabrika Satış** (`c.fabrikaSatisBedeli`), **Komisyon** (`c.komisyon`), **Extra Kalıp** (o makinaya satılan `partSales` tur "Kalıp" `fiyat` toplamı — satır-başı taramadan kaçınmak için tek `extraKalipMap` useMemo). Amaç: Excel'den girilen fiyatları listede aynı hizada görüp yanlış girişi yakalamak. Sütunlar sağa hizalı + hafif turuncu zeminli (`--ambBg3`/`--orTx`). Görünürlük **`appSettings.musteriSutunlari`** `{faturaBedeli, fabrikaSatis, komisyon, extraKalip}` boolean'larına bağlı (varsayılan hepsi kapalı = `MUSTERI_SUTUN_VARSAYILAN`), **Ayarlar > Uygulama > Müşteri Görünümü** (`SettingsMusteri.jsx`, Uygulama grubuna eklendi) ile aç/kapa. Salt görünüm tercihi → yeni izin/serverAuth YOK. Yeni `app_settings.musteriSutunlari TEXT` kolonu **JSON dört-nokta kuralıyla** (`translations`/`servisAlarm` deseni: CREATE + `APP_SETTINGS_MUSTERI_SUTUN_COLUMN` ensureColumns + INSERT `json()` + SELECT `parseJsonCol`). Backup whole-object olduğu için otomatik taşınır. Testler: `db-roundtrip.cjs` (JSON roundtrip), `tests/ui/customers-fiyat-sutunlari.test.jsx`, `tests/ui/settings-musteri.test.jsx`.

### Customers' detail view absorbed three former tabs: "Makina Geçmişi", "Servis ve Yedek Parça", "Extra Kalıp"
All three used to be separate top-level tabs. They were removed one at a time and merged into `Customers.jsx`'s `detailView` modal (opened by clicking a customer/machine row), on the reasoning that once every action is reachable from a customer's own profile, maintaining parallel "all customers" list tabs for the same data is redundant for this app's actual usage. **This was an explicit, deliberate product decision by the user** (not just a refactor convenience) — they were told the trade-off up front (losing cross-customer search like "all services by technician X", and losing a flat at-a-glance "who owes how much" list) and chose to proceed anyway, wanting to see how it feels in practice. If a future request asks to bring back a standalone list tab, that's not a contradiction of earlier work — it's revisiting a knowingly-provisional choice. Key points if touching this:
- `detailViewId` (not the record itself) is the state — `detailView` is derived live via `customers.find(c => c.id === detailViewId)` every render, so it reflects mutations (edits, 2nd-hand transfers, service/kalıp ödeme toggles) immediately instead of going stale like a stored snapshot would.
- The modal is two-column when the firm has more than one machine: a left "Bu Firmanın Makinaları" sidebar (click a row to switch which machine's detail is shown) and a right column with the field grid, "Sahiplik Geçmişi" (ownership chain, if resold), and the combined "Makina Geçmişi" timeline (sale + services + Extra Kalıp sales + warranty-end, built from `services` and `partSales`). With only one machine, the sidebar is hidden and the right column takes the full width.
- There is only ONE edit form for a customer/machine record — `openEdit`/`form`/`save` (the rich one with finance fields, `kaliplar` array, stock-linked serial selection). The old standalone `MachineHistory` tab had a second, simpler, partially-incompatible edit form (different field names: `kalip` singular vs `kaliplar` array, `note` vs `aciklama`) — that was deliberately dropped rather than carried over, to avoid two editors diverging on the same record. The same one-editor principle applies to services (`ServiceForm`) and Extra Kalıp sales (`PartSaleForm`): each has exactly one shared form, opened either fresh (pre-filled with `customerId: detailView.id`) or in edit mode from the timeline, never a second parallel form.
- "Yeni Sahip" (2nd-hand ownership transfer: `saveNewOwner`) and "Yazdır" (`printMachineReport`, the printable "Makina Servis ve Yedek Parça Geçmişi Raporu" for the whole machine) live in `Customers.jsx`, rebased onto `detailView`/`detailHistory`/`detailTimelineEvents`. There's also `printServiceForm(sv)` — the single-service "Servis Formu" printout (ported from the old `Services.jsx`, which had its own per-row Yazdır button); its button sits inline next to that service's title in the timeline, not in the modal's bottom action row, since it's per-record rather than per-machine.
- The "Satış Yapan" field (both in the main edit form and in the "Yeni Sahip" form's "Satan Firma" field) is a quick-pick `<Select>` (Fabrika / Bayiler / Önceki Sahip or Mevcut Sahip) layered over a free-text `<Input>` that holds the actual value — picking an option just fills the input, so any name (e.g. a previous owner who sold privately, not through the factory or a dealer) can also be typed directly.
- The timeline's service AND Extra Kalıp ("Kalıp Verildi") entries are actionable, not just read-only: the "Makina Geçmişi" box header has "Yeni Servis Talebi" and "Extra Kalıp Satışı" buttons (open `ServiceForm`/`PartSaleForm` pre-filled with that customer); each timeline entry's title is clickable to reopen the matching form in edit mode for that record; and the servis ücreti (one `odendi` flag covers both the service fee and the parts fee; there is no separate parts toggle), Extra Kalıp and yedek parça lines each have a one-click "Ödendi"/"Ödenmedi" toggle (`toggleServisOdendi`/`togglePartSaleOdendi`/`toggleYedekParcaOdendi`). Marking paid opens the spec 0044 hesap window when the user has `kasaYetki` and there is an amount of ours to collect; otherwise it flips the flag directly.
- `Customers` needs `partSales`/`setPartSales` and `parts` as props (timeline/print report/Extra Kalıp form, and the "Değişen Parçalar" picker in the embedded `ServiceForm`); `App.jsx`'s `customers` tab render call passes all of them.
- Debt detection is unified across all four sources (customer's own `kalanBorc`, unpaid service fee, unpaid parça fee, unpaid Extra Kalıp sale) via `customerHasAnyDebt(customer, services, partSales)` in `lib/utils.js` (built on `isServisBorcluMu`/`isPartSaleBorcluMu`, also shared). `Customers.jsx`'s "₺ Borçlu Firmalar" filter pill and its row highlight (`hasDebt`, yellow background) both use it — a customer can show up there purely from an unpaid service or mold sale, with no `kalanBorc` at all. `Dashboard.jsx`'s borçlu-firma count and its "Borçlu Firmalar" modal use the same three predicates, and the modal's single "Müşterilerde Görüntüle" button now routes to `Customers` with the debt filter (there's nowhere else left to route to, since the Services/Parts tabs are gone).
- **Müşteri silme kaskadı (`Customers.jsx` `confirmDel` + `src/lib/musteriKaskad.js`):** a customer goes to the Çöp Kutusu with ONE shared `deletedAt` stamp together with its services (their parts restored to stock), Extra Kalıp sales, payments, **yedek parça satışları whose buyer is that customer** (`aliciTipi:"musteri"` — stock restored via `yedekParcaGeriAl`, re-deducted on trash restore), **görüşmeler and dosyalar**. A dealer-bought sale allocated to that machine is NOT deleted (dealer still owes); its tahsis row becomes free text (`customerId:null`, `makinaSerbest:"AD · seri (silinen müşteri)"` via `silinenMakinaEtiketi`). The machine itself is not deleted, it returns to Makina Stoğu (note `Silinen müşteriden geri döndü`); **trash restore removes that returned stock row again** (`geriDonenStokBul`, matched by model + serial + note) and relinks its `makina_uretimi` log rows back to `sourceStockId` — before this the machine sat in both the customer and the stock after a restore. The ConfirmDialog lists live linked-record counts (`musteriBagliSayilar`/`bagliKayitOzeti`). `SettingsTrash` restore/purge/emptyTrash mirror the same stamp-equality rule (`kaskadCocuk`/`kaskadYedekParca`). Server side, `serverAuth.cjs` `kaskadSilmeMi` lets a user holding only `cust_delete` stamp these children in the same write (child-delete ids like `cust_service_delete` are NOT required when the parent customer is deleted in that write; if the customer isn't being deleted, or was already in trash, the normal per-child rule applies), and `yazmaYetkisiVar` accepts `yedekParcaSilebilir` or `cust_delete` at section level too. Known limit (pre-existing, accepted): a user whose admin explicitly set `stockActions: []` cannot write `partStock`/`partStockLog`/`stock`, so for them the cascade's stock restore / machine-return-to-stock 403s — same as the older service-parts restore; a customer user normally has `stockActions` undefined (= unrestricted), which passes. Covered end-to-end in `scripts/tests/server-security.cjs` (`silici` / `siliciStoksuz` users). Teklif/proforma/fatura/üretim formu are documents and deliberately don't cascade. History: yedek parça was missing from the cascade for months → orphan "—" rows in the aylık rapor (LEZZET BAHÇESİ / BAL KÖFTE&BURGER); those pre-existing orphans are NOT fixed by the cascade. **Sahipsiz (orphan) kayıtlar** are handled by `src/lib/sahipsiz.js`: (1) `sahipsizHaric` — **Finance (on-screen) and `hesaplaAylikRapor` both drop customer-linked records (services/partSales/yedekParcaSatislar with `aliciTipi:"musteri"`/payments) whose customer is not among the live customers** (user decision: no "—" rows in the report; dealer/dış-firma spare-part sales have no customer link and always stay); the report returns `sahipsizAdet` and prints a small footer note when > 0 — so `Finance.jsx`'s `aylikRapor()` MUST hand the engine the RAW arrays (`servicesHam` etc.), not the screen-filtered ones, or the note can never appear (that regression was caught by driving the real app; `finance-sahipsiz.test.jsx` guards it). Finance and the report MUST keep using the same helper so numbers don't diverge. (2) `sahipsizKayitlar` feeds **Ayarlar > Veri Yönetimi > Sahipsiz Kayıtlar** (`SettingsSahipsiz.jsx`, settings item id `sahipsiz`): lists orphans (any customer incl. trashed counts as a match there) with "Müşteriye Bağla" (rewrites `customerId`/`musteriId` + tahsis `customerId`) and "Sil" (soft-delete + `yedekParcaGeriAl`/`servisParcaGeriAl` stock restore). **Bayi silme kaskadı (`SimpleDealers.jsx` `confirmDel` + `src/lib/bayiKaskad.js`)** mirrors the customer one: a dealer goes to trash with ONE stamp together with the yedek parça satışları whose buyer is that dealer (`aliciTipi:"bayi"` or legacy `aliciTipi`-less rows; dış firma excluded; stock restored via `yedekParcaGeriAl`, allocations go with the sale as child rows) and its dealer files (`dosyalar[].dealerId`, no `customerId`). The ConfirmDialog lists counts AND an open-receivable warning for unpaid sales (`bayiBagliSayilar.acikAlacak`). Name-linked history (service `islemFirma`, `satisYapan`) is text and deliberately untouched. `SettingsTrash` restore/purge/emptyTrash mirror it (purge also removes the physical dealer files). Server: `kaskadSilmeMi` has a dealer leg (`dealer_delete` + dealer deleted in the same write), `yazmaYetkisiVar` accepts `dealer_delete` for the yedek parça section, and **dealer file records are now authorized with the dealer group** (`EYLEM_IDLERI.dosyalar.bayi` → `dealer_dosya_add/del`, matching `dosyaSilmeYetkisi` on the HTTP file endpoint; before, the blob path always demanded `cust_dosya_*`), with a narrow section-level allowance when every changed dosyalar record is a dealer file (`dosyalarYalnizBayiMi`). `sahipsiz.js` also knows the dealer link (`bayiBagi`, tür `yedekParcaBayi`; `sahipsizHaric(customers, veri, dealers)` third arg), so dealer-less bayi sales are excluded from Finance/report and appear in the Sahipsiz Kayıtlar tool with "Bayiye Bağla". Tests: `bayi-kaskad.test.js`, `ui/dealers-delete-cascade.test.jsx`, `ui/settings-trash.test.jsx`, `sahipsiz.test.js`, `ui/settings-sahipsiz.test.jsx`, `server-authz.test.js`, `server-security.cjs` (`bayiSilici`). Tests: `musteri-kaskad.test.js`, `ui/customers-delete-cascade.test.jsx`, `ui/settings-trash.test.jsx`, `server-authz.test.js`.
- `App.jsx`'s `TABS` array no longer has `services`/`parts`/`machines` entries; `Dashboard.jsx` no longer takes `onGoServices`/`onGoParts` props (removed along with the tabs they pointed to). The "Servis Kayıtları" stat card on the Dashboard is now a plain non-clickable count (no tab to navigate to).
- `src/App.jsx` (last in the chain): owns all top-level state (`customers`, `dealers`, `services`, `stock`, `notes`, `parts`, `partSales`, `kalipDefs`, `standardModels`/`customModels`, `factory`, `appSettings`, geo data, toast, current `tab`) and renders the sidebar + the active tab's component, passing data/setters down as props (no Context, no Redux/Zustand — plain prop drilling).

### Data persistence pattern
- On mount, `App` calls `window.crmStorage.load()`. In local mode this reads from SQLite via `electron/db.cjs`; in client mode it fetches from the server via `electron/ipc/data.cjs`. If Electron APIs are absent (e.g. `npm run preview`), storage silently no-ops and data only lives in memory.
- Any change to the tracked state arrays triggers a `useRef`-backed 500ms debounced `window.crmStorage.save(...)` writing the entire state blob at once. **Saves are serialized through `src/lib/kayitSirasi.js` (`kayitSirasiOlustur`)**: the next save waits for the in-flight one and `__dataVersion` is stamped at send time from `dataVersionRef` (the body captured at state-change time carries a stale version). Without this, a second state change landing while a save was in flight (e.g. first-time auto-backup: folder pick save in flight → backup written → `lastBackup` set) went out with the old version and was rejected as a fake conflict ("Değişiklikler kaydedilemedi!" + needless merge). Real external changes still conflict. `flushSave` on unload also re-stamps the version. Guarded by `tests/kayit-sirasi.test.js` + `tests/ui/app-kayit-sirasi.test.jsx`. **The blob is the wire/IPC format only — SQLite does NOT store it as a blob.** `db.cjs` unpacks it into normalized, column-per-field tables (`customers`, `services`, `dealers`, …); the `meta` table holds only small key/value scalars (`dataVersion` etc.). Consequence: **adding a field to a record is not enough to persist it.** A new column must be added in four places or the value is silently dropped on the next load — `SCHEMA_SQL`, an `ensureColumns` entry in `applyColumnMigrations`, the `INSERT` column list + params, and the read/SELECT mapping. This has bitten before (`factory.ilce`); a roundtrip test in `scripts/tests/db-roundtrip.cjs` is the guard.
- SQLite writes are atomic via WAL mode; `electron/db.cjs` also keeps the old `data.json` atomic-write pattern (`fs.renameSync`) for the JSON fallback path.
- Schema migrations: `SCHEMA_SQL` holds the `CREATE TABLE` baseline, but newer columns are added incrementally via `ensureColumns` (idempotent). `migrateFromJsonIfNeeded` has three init branches — existing DB, clean install, and JSON→SQLite import — and **all three must call `applyColumnMigrations(conn)` after `SCHEMA_SQL`** (that function bundles every `ensureColumns` call). Regression to avoid: clean install used to run `SCHEMA_SQL` only, so a brand-new machine's first session was missing later columns (e.g. `uretim_formlari.baslangicTarihi`) and writing such a record crashed until the second launch. When you add a new column, add it to `applyColumnMigrations` (not just one branch); `scripts/tests/db-clean-install.cjs` guards the clean-install path.
- Multi-user mode: server PC runs `electron/server.cjs` (Express + JWT). Client PCs poll `/api/version` every 10 s and listen for `server:dataChanged` push events; spec 0077: both polls and the push reload only when `kayitDurumu.veriYenilenebilirMi` allows it (no pending/in-flight own save, 500 ms after it, not during the load suppression). Optimistic locking with `dataVersion` prevents silent overwrites (409 on conflict → reload). Entity-level pessimistic locking via `useLock` hook + `/api/lock` endpoint (2-min TTL, heartbeat every 60 s, fail-open). Records that are reachable from several screens share ONE lock area so they can't be edited from two places at once: a yedek parça (kargo) satışı locks `("yedek_parca", saleId)` from the Stok edit form, the **makinaya tahsis modal (`TahsisModal`)**, and the board's kargo detay modal; a service locks `("customer", customerId)` from both the customer detail and the board's service edit form; Extra Kalıp locks `("part_sale", saleId)`. **Servis ve Kargo Panosu box-drag** is an instantaneous action, so it does NOT hold a `useLock` (a held lock doesn't map to a drag); instead the board keeps a live lock list (`window.crmLocks.list()` refreshed on `server:locksChanged`) and, on drop, refuses the move + toasts if the dragged card's record is currently locked by **another** user (`baskasiKilitli` in `ServisPanosu.jsx`, keyed by the same three lock areas; own-user locks don't block). This is a soft guard on top of the optimistic `dataVersion` 409.
- Permissions are enforced on the server too, not just in the UI. The `makeCanDo`/`READONLY_SERVER_PERMISSIONS` model in `src/lib/permissions.js` hides buttons client-side; `POST /api/data` additionally re-checks it (`electron/serverAuth.cjs`): it diffs the incoming blob against the stored one section-by-section (`degisenBolumler`, key-order-stable) and rejects (403) any changed section the user's permission group blocks — so a crafted HTTP request from a read-only/restricted client can't overwrite data it can't edit in the UI. `BLOB_SECTIONS` lives in `serverAuth.cjs` (single source; `db.cjs` imports it) and every section must be mapped in `SECTION_GROUP` (a test enforces coverage). Limitation by design: the whole-blob save model can't distinguish add/edit/delete, so a group that is non-empty-but-partial (e.g. edit-but-not-delete) is trusted at the section level. **Two exceptions are enforced record-by-record in `eylemDenetimi`:** (1) ADD/DELETE — an unauthorized new/removed id is rejected via `EYLEM_IDLERI`; (2) **specific-field edits** — a curated `ALAN_IZINLERI` map re-checks a permission when ONE watched field changes, so **Kanban box-drag (status change) is server-enforced** for all three card types: `services.durum` requires `cust_service_edit`, `partSales.kargoDurum` (Extra Kalıp kargo card) requires `cust_kalip_edit`, `yedekParcaSatislar.kargoDurum` (yedek parça kargo card) requires `yedek_parca_edit`. Only that field is watched, so unrelated toggles (`ödendi`, `kargoSorumlusu`, `panoGizli`) stay section-level and don't false-positive. New records (id absent from the old blob) fall to the ADD branch, not the field branch. Guarded in `server-authz.test.js` + end-to-end in `server-security.cjs`. Full/admin users skip the diff (`kisitliMi` gate). This matters most once the server is reachable beyond the LAN (e.g. over a Tailscale VPN).
- **Express 5 (body-parser 2):** a bodyless request leaves `req.body` **undefined** (Express 4 gave `{}`), so every handler destructures `req.body || {}`; body-parser errors keep their own status through the global error handler (bad JSON 400, over-limit 413, Turkish message, no internals). The e2e script `scripts/tests/server-security.cjs` records every request it makes and requires **every `app.<method>("path")` route in `server.cjs` to be called at least once** (uç kapsamı check): a new route without an e2e call fails the test. `scripts/tests/istemci-sunucu.cjs` (`tests/istemci-sunucu.test.js`, Electron) drives the REAL client IPC code (`electron/ipc/data.cjs`: login with TLS trust and pin, load/save with reason codes, conflict, locks, file bridge, user admin, refresh, logout) against the real server with separate server/client userData folders. The old `body-parser ^1.20.6` override was removed (it forced 1.x under Express 5).
- **Rate limiting on the embedded server (three layers):** (1) a global `express-rate-limit` on every `/api` route (IP-based, 600 req/min, 429 with a Turkish `error` + `RateLimit` header; in-memory, resets on restart) — added mainly to cover CodeQL's `js/missing-rate-limiting` with a library it recognizes; `/health` and `/auth` are outside it; (2) the custom escalating brute-force lock on `/auth/login` (`rateLimit.cjs`, persisted in DB); (3) the per-user 60/min write limit on `POST /api/data`. The e2e script checks the global limiter LAST (601 requests → 429) because the window then blocks `/api` for a minute. **GitHub Code scanning (CodeQL default setup, JS + Actions)** is enabled on the repo; `pinnedFetch.cjs`'s `rejectUnauthorized:false` is a documented false positive (TOFU fingerprint fetch, dismissed on GitHub); workflows carry `permissions: contents: read`.
- Two server-authoritative logs, both admin-only, both pruned after 12 months: **security log** (`security_log`, "Kullanıcı Geçmişi", via `logSecurity` in `server.cjs`) and **audit log** (`audit_log`, "İşlem Geçmişi", via `db.writeAuditEntry`). Security log records auth/user-management events plus (added later): `sifre_degistirildi` (`PATCH /auth/me/password`), `cikis` (`POST /auth/logout`, called by the client's `server:logout` IPC before dropping the token), `oturum_yenilendi` (`GET /auth/me`, ~daily), `2fa_kurulum_baslatildi` (`POST /auth/2fa/setup`), `kilit_devralindi`/`kilit_birakildi` (force-steal / release on the lock endpoints). Server-PC-only local admin ops are logged straight to `security_log` from the IPC layer (not via HTTP) through `logYerelGuvenlik` (`ipc/data.cjs`) / `kaydetYerelGuvenlik` (`ipc/system.cjs`) — actor = config username, ip = `"yerel"`, no-op when the local DB is inactive (client PCs): `ilk_admin_olusturuldu`, `sunucu_baslatildi`/`sunucu_durduruldu`, `tls_modu_degistirildi`, `sertifika_yenilendi`, `sunucu_ayari_silindi`, `yedek_alindi`, `yedek_parolasi_degistirildi`, `uygulama_kilidi_kuruldu`/`_kaldirildi`/`_parolasi_degistirildi`.
- The audit log is otherwise **client-driven** — the client POSTs `/api/audit` with self-declared `action/entity` per user action (`src/lib/audit.js`, 61 call sites). To close the "crafted HTTP write leaves no trace" gap, `POST /api/data` now writes a server-authoritative **`veri_kaydedildi`** row for **every** write (admin included), entity `"sunucu"`, `entity_name` = changed section labels from `degisenBolumler` via `BOLUM_ADLARI`. This reads the stored blob on every write (the admin perf-skip of the authz diff no longer applies). File ops audit server-side too: `POST /api/files/upload` → `yuklendi`/`dosya`, `DELETE /api/files/:name` → `silindi`/`dosya`. Any new action/entity string must be added to the label maps in `SettingsSecurityLog.jsx`/`SettingsAuditLog.jsx` or it renders as its raw key; `SettingsSecurityLog` also surfaces the failed-login reason (`detail.sebep`) inline next to the `giris_basarisiz` label, and `SettingsAuditLog` now shows a Rol column. Covered end-to-end in `scripts/tests/server-security.cjs`. **Harness gotcha:** under Electron the check script's `process.exit(1)` is deferred (execution continues), so the failure path `return`s before printing the `TUM KONTROLLER GECTI` sentinel — the vitest wrapper asserts both exit 0 AND that sentinel, so a failed check can't slip through as a pass.
- Manual backup/restore and scheduled auto-backup go through separate IPC channels (`crm:backup`, `crm:restore`, `crm:chooseFolder`, `crm:writeBackup`) and write timestamped JSON files.
- New records get IDs from the global `uid()` counter, not from `crypto.randomUUID()` or DB-assigned IDs — when loading saved data, `bumpId(...)` scans existing records to advance the counter past any existing max ID.

### Faaliyet Haritası (the map tab)
- `src/components/Harita.jsx` + `src/components/harita/HaritaSvg.jsx` + `src/lib/mapStats.js`. Three drill levels: Dünya → ülke/il → ilçe. It's an "activity" map: every place that has sales is filled a **single logo orange** (`--hSatis`, not a density ramp) and gets a neutral location pin (`tur:"satis"`, theme-aware `--n900` so it stands out from the orange fill and the blue/green dealer pins) with the place name as a label; the factory and dealers/services are the other pins. **Every drawn place is name-labeled**, selling or not: selling ones via their satis pin, non-selling ones via a muted standalone label at the shape centroid (`yolMerkezi(d)` in `mapStats.js`). Labels live in the pin layer so they stay screen-px sized (readable when zoomed in, overlapping when zoomed out — accepted).
- **Map geometry is generated at build time, not fetched at runtime.** `scripts/gen-map-paths.cjs` turns upstream boundary data into plain JS modules under `src/lib/map/` (~8MB, **committed on purpose** — the app must work offline and the generator needs network + a local cache). Don't hand-edit anything in `src/lib/map/`; change the generator and re-run it. Companion: `scripts/gen-arkaplan-adlar.cjs` → `src/lib/map/world-arkaplan.js` (`ARKA_PLAN_AD` = Turkish names + projected centroids for the ~92 world countries **outside** the 86-country sales list, which `world.js`'s `ARKA_PLAN` stores nameless; territories below a projected-area threshold are skipped to avoid clutter, except a `KUCUK_ULKE` allowlist of small sovereign states (Luxembourg, Malta, Monaco, Andorra, Vatican, small island nations…) which are labeled regardless of size; dependent territories and geographic features stay excluded). It's **offline** — only `world-atlas` (local) + `d3-geo` (devDep) — and must reproduce `gen-map-paths.cjs`'s exact world projection + used-country set or the labels drift off the paths.
- Those modules are lazy-loaded via `import.meta.glob`, so Vite code-splits them and the main bundle only grows ~43KB. `d3-geo`/`topojson-*` are **devDependencies only** (build-time); never import them from renderer code.
- **Licensing matters here.** Sources: Natural Earth (public domain), GeoNames (CC BY), and geoBoundaries TUR ADM1+ADM2 (**ODbL 1.0 — share-alike**, pinned to a specific commit). Attribution is surfaced in-app under Ayarlar > Uygulama > Harita Veri Kaynakları (`SettingsApp.jsx` `HARITA_KAYNAKLARI`), which is the only attribution place (there is no `ATTRIBUTION.md` in the repo, spec 0094 R11). If you swap or add a source, update that accordion.
- **District (ilçe) breakdown covers nineteen provinces (on dokuz il, spec 0094, commit `4b60c02`, plan `specs/done/0094-uygulama-plani.md`):** the list is `ILCE_ILLERI` in `scripts/gen-map-paths.cjs` and the same generated list (`src/lib/map/ilceler.js`) also feeds the İlçe field of the customer, dealer, two delivery-address and factory forms, so a province not on the list cannot have an ilçe entered at all. Adding a province: its name must match in three dictionaries (`CITIES_TR`, geoBoundaries `shapeName`, the runtime city list; "Hakkari"/"Hakkâri" differs), add it, re-run `node scripts/gen-map-paths.cjs` (the cache under `scripts/.map-cache/` makes it reproducible; first run once untouched and confirm `git status --short src/lib/map` is empty), and expect only `ilce/*`, `ilceler.js`, `meta.js` to change. Do NOT run `gen-arkaplan-adlar.cjs` for this. The generator deletes `ilce/` before validating the names; recovery is `git checkout -- src/lib/map/ilce`. Name fixes go in `ILCE_ISIM_DUZELT` ("Yalova merkez" → "Yalova"; "Çanakkale merkez" deliberately left, records may hold it). **Unmatched ilçe values** (a filled `ilce` not in the province's list, only possible via XLSX import) are not painted, get no pin and are counted in the province panel (`ilOzeti(customers, il, ILCELER[il])` → `eslesmeyen`, `ilceEslesmeyenMetni`; pin rule via `pinleriTopla({ ilceAdlari })`; both opt-in, without the list the output is the old one).
- Gotchas already paid for: pin scale must be normalized to screen px (`sigdirmaOlcegi()`) or pins shrink on tall viewBoxes; polygon winding is fixed by measuring area > 2π and reversing; districts come from geoBoundaries ADM1 (not Natural Earth provinces, which misfile Tuzla under Kocaeli).

### Servis Panosu (the "servis" tab / service-floor kiosk)
- **Ayrı pencere (pop-out to a 2nd monitor), like Faaliyet Haritası but BIDIRECTIONAL.** The board can open in its own `BrowserWindow` (`servis.html` entry → `src/servis-main.jsx` → `src/components/ServisPencere.jsx`; `electron/preload-servis.cjs`; `electron/ipc/servis-pencere.cjs`; `main.cjs` `servisWin`; vite input `servis`). Unlike the read-only map window, this one is fully interactive, so the wrapper is a **two-way bridge**: (1) data flows down — `App` pushes a snapshot via `appServisPano.veriPush` (main-window preload) → `servis:veriPush` cached + forwarded → `ServisPencere` pulls (`servis:ilkVeriAl`) + lives (`servis:veri`), same race-safe pull+forward as the map; (2) writes flow up — every board setter (`setServices`/`setYedekParcaSatislar`/`setPartSales`/`setPartStock`/`setPartStockLog`/`setDosyalar`) is a shim in `ServisPencere` that applies the updater to its local mirror and sends the full resulting array as `servis:mutate {key,value}` → main process → `App.onMutate` applies `setX(value)`. **The main window stays the SOLE owner of state + persistence** (`App` is NOT mounted in the servis window — two savers would 409 each other, same reason `harita-main` avoids it). **Load-bearing subtlety:** the pushed snapshot sends the WRITABLE arrays in **FULL** (soft-deleted rows included) as the updater base, while `ServisPencere` renders `withoutDeleted(...)` for display — so a drag maps over the full array and the value returned to `setX` preserves soft-deleted records (dropping them would be silent data loss). Read-only arrays (customers/dealers/parts/calisanlar) are pushed `live`. `preload-servis.cjs` exposes only what the board needs (`servisBridge` + `crmLocks`/`appServer.apiRequest`+`onLocksChanged`/`auditLog`/`appFiles`/`appPrint`) — those IPC channels are window-agnostic so locks/audit/print/files work directly from the pop-out. The "⧉ Ayrı Pencere" header button shows only in the main-window tab (`onAyriPencere` prop; not passed in kiosk or inside the pop-out itself). Guarded by `tests/servis-veri-deposu.test.js` (cache store) + `tests/ui/servis-pencere-veri.test.jsx` (bridge render + drag→mutate + soft-delete preservation + theme). Not coupled to the app-lock (the map window has the same characteristic).
- `src/components/ServisPanosu.jsx` — a Kanban board of services by work status **`durum`** (a new plain-TEXT service field: "Bekliyor" / "Yapılıyor" / "Tamamlandı"; **only services that have a `durum` appear** — legacy/durum-less services stay off the board and are managed from the customer detail). Native HTML5 drag-drop (no library) moves a card between columns → `setServices(p => p.map(s => s.id===id ? {...s, durum} : s))`. Each card has a technician `<select>`, and "Yeni Servis Talebi"/card-edit open **the exact same full `ServiceForm` as the customer detail** (explicit user requirement — the board form must be identical to the customer service-request form, parts/money/stock included; a new service defaults to `durum:"Bekliyor"`). Its save replicates `CustomerDetailModal.saveService` (`rec = {...form, customerId, parcaUcretsizMi, parcaUcreti, parcaUcretiAltuntastan, parcaCurrency}`). **Because the form is identical, the parts stock-deduction is too:** the deduct/restore logic is factored into `src/lib/servisStok.js` (`servisParcaDus`/`servisParcaGeriAl`, writing `partStock`/`partStockLog`), shared by both the board and `CustomerDetailModal` so they never drift. The board therefore needs `parts`/`dealers`/`kdvRates`/`geoData`/`loadingGeo`/`setPartStock`/`setPartStockLog` props from `App.jsx`. **The identical form also includes the "Servis Resimleri / Dosyaları" upload** — the board passes `dosyalar`/`setDosyalar`/`dosyaCevrimdisi` and replicates `CustomerDetailModal.bindServisDosyalari` (binds `dosyaTaslaklari` from `onSave`'s 2nd arg to the saved service as `refType:"servis"`); the file section is gated by `canDo("cust_dosya_add")` exactly like the customer form, so it auto-hides for a user without that permission.
- **"Panodan Kaldır" (archive) on completed cards:** a Tamamlandı card has a "🗄 Kaldır" button that sets a new `panoGizli` boolean (INTEGER 0/1 in `db.cjs`, so it follows the four-place rule *with* explicit `toInt`/`toBool` — a boolean can't ride the `...rest` auto-read). This **hides the card from the board without deleting the service** — the record stays in customer detail / machine history / reports, and `durum` stays `"Tamamlandı"` so the history badge is intact. Archived cards are reachable via a collapsible "🗄 Arşivlenenler (N)" reveal under the Tamamlandı column, each with a "↩ Panoya Geri Al" (`panoGizli:false`). Dragging an archived-then-restored card, or moving any card out of Tamamlandı, clears `panoGizli` (so it can't stay hidden in a non-completed column). The board filters `panoGizli` out of its columns; setting it is a plain `services` edit, so no serverAuth change is needed (already mapped to `"servis"` via the services section).
- **Technician is a dropdown of company employees**, not free text. Employees are a small `{id, ad}` list `calisanlar`, stored via the **meta-JSON pattern** (like `partTypeDefs`; `db.cjs` write/read near partTypeDefs, no column migration), managed in Ayarlar > Firma > Firma Çalışanları (`CalisanManager.jsx`, built on `useSimpleDefList`). `ServiceForm`'s "Teknisyen" field is also this select now (needs the `calisanlar` prop, drilled App→Customers→CustomerDetailModal→ServiceForm). `services[].tech` still stores the plain name, so `CalisanManager` renames propagate via `onRename`.
- **Full kiosk** for the service-floor PC: it's a server **client** logged in as a restricted `user` with `permissions.tabs:["servis"]` (+ `customerActions:["cust_service_add","cust_service_edit"]`). When a client's only visible tab is `servis` (`kioskMode` in `App.jsx`), the whole shell (sidebar, search, tab nav) is skipped and only the board renders fullscreen. **Critical server coupling:** `electron/serverAuth.cjs` `BOLUM_SEKMELERI.services` MUST include `"servis"` (else the kiosk's status writes 403 via `sekmeEngelli`); **and because the board's service form deducts parts and can attach files, `BOLUM_SEKMELERI.partStock`/`partStockLog`/`dosyalar` also include `"servis"`** (else a parts-carrying or file-carrying service save 403s for the kiosk user). Attaching files on the kiosk additionally needs `cust_dosya_add` in the user's `customerActions` (the section otherwise stays hidden, so no 403 in practice). The kiosk user's `stockActions` must stay unset/undefined (its default) — that's the group gate; UserManager only writes a `stockActions` array if the admin explicitly restricts it, so a plain `tabs:["servis"]` + `customerActions` user passes. `calisanlar` is a new BLOB_SECTIONS entry mapped to `settings` in SECTION_GROUP/BOLUM_SEKMELERI; and `servis` is in `serverPermissionDefs.js` `ALL_TABS` so UserManager shows the checkbox. Adding `durum` follows the usual four-place DB rule (SELECT auto for plain TEXT); `panoGizli` is INTEGER so its SELECT mapping is explicit (`toBool`).
- **Aşama zaman takibi (time tracking):** three plain-TEXT service fields hold local `"YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss"` stamps — `fabrikaGirisZamani` (Bekliyor entry), `bakimBaslangicZamani` (Yapılıyor entry), `bitisZamani` (Tamamlandı entry). Stamped in `ServisPanosu.durumDegistir` (drag-drop) and at creation (`kaydet` add-branch + `CustomerDetailModal.saveService`): giriş/başlangıç are set ONLY IF empty (idempotent — first time is preserved across back-and-forth drags), bitiş is overwritten each entry to Tamamlandı. Editable manually via three `datetime-local` fields in `ServiceForm` (shown only when `form.durum` set). Cards show: entry time (Bekliyor), a **live labor counter** driven by the board's 1s `now` clock (Yapılıyor), end time + labor duration (Tamamlandı). A per-card 📊 opens a per-service **analysis Modal** (timeline + bekleme/işçilik/toplam durations, live if ongoing). Pure calc in `src/lib/servisAnaliz.js` (`servisSureleri(sv, nowIso, calismaSaatleri)`) + helpers in `utils.js` (`simdiYerel`/`sureDk`/`sureBicim`/`fmtZaman`); `MachineTimeline` shows an ⏱ labor badge on completed services. Four-place DB rule (SELECT auto, plain TEXT); roundtrip-tested.
  - **İşçilik = mesai (working-hours) süresi, not wall-clock.** Labor (`isclikDk`) counts ONLY minutes falling inside the firm's working windows via `utils.mesaiDk(bas, bit, cs)` — nights, non-working days and breaks are excluded (else a Fri-evening→Mon service billed ~65h). **Bekleme and Toplam stay raw wall-clock** (real elapsed); only İşçilik is clamped, and `servisSureleri` also returns `isclikHamDk` (raw) for reference. The config lives in **`appSettings.calismaSaatleri`** `{ baslangic, bitis, gunler:[getDay 0=Paz..6=Cmt], molalar:[{baslangic,bitis}] }` (default `CALISMA_SAATLERI_VARSAYILAN` in `constants.js` = 08:30–19:00 Pzt–Cum, öğle 12:30–13:30), edited in **Ayarlar > Firma > Çalışma Saatleri** (`SettingsCompany.jsx` — adjustable hours, day chips, multi-break list). Stored as one JSON column in `app_settings` (`APP_SETTINGS_CALISMA_COLUMN`, four-place JSON rule like translations). Consumers pass the config through: `ServisPanosu` (has `appSettings`), `MachineTimeline` (drilled `calismaSaatleri` from `CustomerDetailModal`'s `appSettings`). `mesaiDk` iterates day-by-day building windows from date parts (no TZ/DST drift; Turkey has no DST), clamps negatives to 0, null-safe like `sureDk`. Tested in `tests/mesai.test.js`.

### Printing and export
- Printable documents (machine history report, service form) are built as raw HTML strings via template literals (not JSX) inside the relevant component, then sent through `window.appPrint.printHtml(html)`, which opens a visible Electron `BrowserWindow` preview with a Print/Close toolbar injected into the page (see `app:printHtml` handler in `main.cjs`). There's a browser-mode fallback (open as blob / download `.html`) when `window.appPrint` isn't available.
- **Print translations are per-document (`appSettings.translations`).** Teklif and Proforma are now edited independently (`SettingsTranslations.jsx` has NO shared "Ortak" section — the shared groups are re-emitted under each doc via `ortakGruplar(ns)`). Storage shape: `{ teklif:{TR,EN}, proforma:{TR,EN}, servis, makina, sandik, fatura }`. **The merge order in `buildPrintHtml` is load-bearing and must stay `DEFAULT < flat translations.TR/.EN (legacy shared pool) < translations[ns]` (`ceviriNs = isProforma ? "proforma" : "teklif"`)** — dropping the flat layer silently reverts existing users' customizations. `servis/makina/sandik/fatura` were already namespaced and are unaffected. `ceviri-kapsam.test.js` guards servis/makina key coverage; `print-templates.test.js` guards the teklif/proforma namespace + legacy fallback.
- **Proforma No (like the yurt dışı fatura's invoice no):** the proforma prints its `form.no` (year-based per-type sequence via `nextDocNo`, same as teklif) in the header badge AND the belge-info block (`L.proformaNoLabel` = "Proforma No"; EN proforma shows it in the FROM block). It's **editable in Evrak > Belge Detayları** (`Documents.jsx` `proforma_keys` includes `"no"`, label type-aware). **Adding a belge field is a two-place change:** the `*_keys` list in `Documents.jsx` AND `BUILTIN_BELGE.<type>` in `SettingsDocuments.jsx` (else the field renders but can't be hidden/reordered in Ayarlar > Evrak & Süreçler). `no` is NOT in `PROFORMA_SYNC_FIELDS`, so a proforma keeps its own number when its parent teklif is edited. Guarded in `print-templates.test.js` + `documents-defaults.test.js`.
- **Veri Yönetimi note:** JSON backup/restore (`SettingsBackup.jsx`) is whole-object, so new record fields ride along with no code change. The **CSV/XLSX export reports** (`SettingsExport.jsx`) are curated column lists, so a new field only appears if added there. The **Finans Özeti** export totals come from the pure `finansOzetiHesapla` (same file): service fee counts only for `isServisUcretliMi` services (Altuntaş, ücretli tip) and its KDV is added ON TOP via `calcKDV` (fees are entered KDV-hariç, Faturalı Yurtiçi only); Altuntaş parça (own service) and anlaşmalı parça (external-firm service, `altuntasParcaBedeli`) have their own lines + KDV, so the summary equals the report's BAKIM ONARIM section (`servisNet`/`servisBolumKdv`); rows come from the pure `finansOzetiSatirlari`. Unknown currencies fold into the TL bucket (the report keeps them keyed) — the same rule as Finance/aylık rapor; it used to assume KDV-dahil fees (`extractKDV`) and count external-firm services, so Excel and the report disagreed on the same service (`tests/finans-ozeti-export.test.js` cross-checks against `hesaplaAylikRapor`). The yedek parça (kargo) export row is the pure `yedekParcaExportRow` (resolves buyer via `aliciAd`/`aliciRozet` so anlaşmasız dış firma shows correctly; includes Teslim Şekli + Farklı Teslimat Adresi), tested in `yedek-parca-export.test.js`.
- **Yurt Dışı Fatura packaging note** lives in its own `paketlemeNot` form-config section (split from `paketleme` in `SettingsDocuments.jsx`; `gocFaturaNot` migrates old hidden/order/label state on mount). `buildFaturaHtml` hides the note when EITHER `hiddenFields.paketlemeNot` OR (legacy) `hiddenFields.paketleme` contains `"not"`, so un-migrated configs still hide it.
- **UserManager** surfaces Servis Panosu permissions in their own "Servis Panosu işlemleri" accordion, but they are still the `customerActions` `cust_service_*` ids (no separate server permission dimension) — the accordion shares the `customerActions` customize toggle with "Müşteri işlemleri". The split filters `CUSTOMER_ACTION_GROUPS` by the **`servisPano: true` flag** on the group (not by group name; guarded by `server-permission-defs.test.js`). Kasa işlemleri get the same treatment from `giderActions` (spec 0066, below). Settings left-menu groups (`SETTINGS_GROUPS`) were reorganized gen-crm-style: Sunucu is its own group (out of Güvenlik), plus Uygulama/Firma/Katalog/Evrak & Süreçler/Veri Yönetimi.
- CSV export is done manually (BOM + `;`-delimited rows) via `downloadCSV()`-style helpers in `Settings`.
- XLSX export/import uses the `xlsx` (SheetJS) package directly in `Settings` for full-data import/export templates.
- **Aylık Faaliyet Raporu** (PDF, from Finance): pure engine `src/lib/aylikRapor.js` (`hesaplaAylikRapor`) → `buildAylikRaporHtml` in `printTemplates.js`. The engine takes a `veri` object (`{customers, services, partSales, payments, teklifler, dealers, yedekParcaSatislar}`) and must use the SAME revenue rules as the on-screen Finance (Altuntaş servisi / ücretli tip / Altuntaş parçası / çek ancak tahsil edilince) so the two never diverge. **Adding a new revenue/receivable source is a four-place change here too:** pass it into `veri` (`Finance.jsx`'s `aylikRapor()`), add its buckets in `hesaplaAylikRapor` (fold net into `ciroNet`, KDV into `toplamKdv`/`kdvKalemleri`, unpaid into `alacak`/`alacakMap`), return the new fields, and render them in `buildAylikRaporHtml`. Yedek parça (kargo) satışları (`yedekParcaSatislar`) are wired this way: müşteri buyer → parça ücreti side, bayi/dış firma buyer → anlaşmalı/bayi parça side (matching Finance), with a firma-firma detay table; buyer name via `kargoAlici` (needs `dealers`). `borcluFirma` now = `alacakMap.size` (distinct debtors across all sources incl. non-customer bayi/dış-firma kargo debtors keyed `b:`/`x:`), not just `customerHasAnyDebt` count. Tahsilat also has a `tahsilatYontemKirilimi` (Nakit/Havale/Çek… breakdown). Covered by `tests/aylik-rapor.test.js` + `tests/print-templates.test.js`.

### Conventions to preserve when editing
- **Components receive `live*` arrays (trash filtered out) but setters act on the FULL state array.** Always write with a functional updater on the full array (`setPayments(p => p.map(...))`), never `setX(derivedFromLiveProp)`: writing a live list back silently hard-deletes every soft-deleted record in that section (triyaj 2026-09-28: müşteri detayında tahsilat ekleme/düzenleme ve eski çek işareti bütün müşterilerin çöpteki tahsilatlarını siliyordu; `tests/ui/tahsilat-cop-korunur.test.jsx`).
- All user-facing strings and form labels are in Turkish — keep new UI text consistent with this.
- Money fields are stored as raw numbers in state and only formatted for display (`fmtCur`/`fmt`); free-text money inputs go through `parseMoney()` to normalize Turkish thousands/decimal separators (`.`/`,`).
- `normalizeSaleType()` maps legacy sale-type strings to the current four-way `SALE_TYPES` (full Faturalı/Faturasız × Yurtiçi/Yurtdışı matrix: "Faturalı Yurtiçi" / "Faturalı Yurtdışı" / "Faturasız Yurtiçi" / "Faturasız Yurtdışı"). It recognizes already-current values as-is, then falls back to substring matching for legacy data — the old three-way system ("Faturalı Yurt İçi" / "Faturalı İhracat" / plain "Faturasız" with no yurtiçi/yurtdışı qualifier) and very old "Faturalı"/"Faturasız" data. Legacy "Faturasız" (no qualifier) defaults to "Faturasız Yurtiçi" since that data never tracked the distinction. Don't assume sale type fields are always already normalized when reading old/imported data — always run them through `normalizeSaleType()` first.
- When adding a new persisted state array in `App`, remember to (1) seed it in the initial load fallback, (2) include it in the debounced save effect's dependency array and payload, (3) include it in backup export/import and the XLSX template in `Settings` if it should be portable, and (4) **if it's an id-keyed record list, add it to `MERGE_KEYS` in `src/lib/merge.js` AND add an `apply(setX, "x")` line in `App.jsx`'s `mergeLocalIntoReloaded`.** Skipping (4) silently drops that array's just-added records whenever the conflict-merge runs — on a **single server PC** this fires from the app's own 5 s version poll (`calisanlar` disappeared "after a while" exactly this way). `mergeLocalIntoReloaded` only re-applies MERGE_KEYS *adds*; edits to pre-existing records still lose to the reloaded copy, so the save path also syncs `dataVersionRef` right after a successful `save()` to stop our own write from looking like an external change and triggering that reload. **`appSettings` is a singleton (not a MERGE_KEY list)**, and it had the same loss class: a reload's `setAppSettings(s => ({ ...s, ...disAppSettingsSuz(data.appSettings) }))` (App.jsx load) overwrote a local appSettings edit that hadn't round-tripped yet, so on **LAN/multi-PC** a just-toggled setting (e.g. the Analiz "Analiz'de Göster" checkbox = `analizGizliModeller`, or `musteriSutunlari`) reverted after close-open. Fix: `mergeLocalIntoReloaded` now also runs `setAppSettings(prev => mergeAppSettings(prev, myData.appSettings))` — `mergeAppSettings` (pure, in `utils.js`, tested) overlays this PC's local appSettings over the reloaded one (minus machine-local fields via `disAppSettingsSuz`), same "local change wins on merge" philosophy as MERGE_KEYS adds. Any new appSettings sub-field inherits this automatically (rides in the whole-object overlay); no per-field wiring.
